# PROGRESS.md

Build status and change log for Winston Sip and Serve. Updated as a standard part of every Claude Code prompt going forward — see CLAUDE.md's Development Workflow for the process this supports.

---

## Build Status

- Next.js scaffold: Done — TypeScript, Tailwind, App Router, npm
- CLAUDE.md: Done — identity, tech stack, locked architecture decisions (5 resource types), scope/future-extension plan, skills, MCP servers, env var reference, dev workflow, branch promotion policy, open items
- PROJECT_CONTEXT.md: Done — resource inventory, full pricing, guest fee rule, add-on services, membership tiers, membership application/approval flow, cancellation/reschedule policy, admin capabilities, bulletin
- Prisma schema: Done — 16 models, booking exclusion constraint (isolated migration), resource inventory seeded (pricing seeding deferred)
- Customer-facing booking flow: Backend hold/availability logic done; announcement gate + 5-step booking wizard (SPORT/COURT/DATE & TIME/ADD-ONS/SUMMARY) done end-to-end through actual PayMongo payment; SPORT step's 5 resource-type cards no longer show pricing inline — each has a "View Pricing" button that opens a shared `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx` component listing every duration tier for that resource type (courts: one flat-hourly row; simulators: full tier breakdown, e.g. 15/30/60 min for tennis/pickleball sims, 60/90 min for golf sim — same figures as before, just moved off the card — see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry); selected cards/buttons across SPORT, COURT, and DATE & TIME (Duration + Time) now render with a bolder `border-2`/10%-tint/`font-semibold` treatment vs. unselected `border`/5%-tint/`font-medium` (Calendar's own selected-date fill untouched); `AnnouncementGate` now shows an inert (non-clickable) "Already a member? Sign in…" line above its Continue button, placeholder for member auth; **every step in the wizard, including the Payment page, is now on brand tokens** (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entries) — no step is on unthemed `foreground`/`zinc`/`dark:` styling anymore; DATE & TIME step uses a real calendar + time-slot grid backed by `GET /api/availability`, with server-side business-hours enforcement; ADD-ONS step is fully live (Ball Boy/Coaching priced, submitted, and reflected on Summary/confirmation); SUMMARY's Confirm Booking now creates the `pending_payment` hold immediately (non-member provisional pricing, `customerId: null`) and reveals a Payment page showing the Booking Reference right away; Pay Now attaches the real customer and re-prices member-aware via `PATCH /api/bookings/[id]`, with an explicit "Continue to Payment" confirmation step when the final price differs from the provisional one, before auto-redirecting to PayMongo Checkout; the Payment page has no Back button — Start Over (always visible) is the only way to leave without completing payment. The line-item summary shown on SUMMARY and Payment is now one shared `BookingSummary` component (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry) rather than duplicated JSX. The `/api/resources` fetch that gates the wizard's first paint (SPORT step) is now lifted to `BookPage` and starts on `/book` page load rather than on gate-continue-click, so the Supabase round-trip runs concurrently with the `AnnouncementGate` being read rather than after it (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry). SPORT's 5 resource-type cards and SUMMARY's ledger rows now carry small inline-SVG icons (new `src/components/ui/Icons.tsx`, following `SocialIcons.tsx`'s hand-authored-SVG convention) — sport family icons (racket/paddle/golf-flag, shared across each sport's court and simulator card) on SPORT, reactive to selection state; `accent-primary/10` circular badge icons on each SUMMARY ledger row. ADD-ONS and the Payment page were deliberately left out of this icon pass (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry for why Payment couldn't be touched without a prop gate).
- Marketing site (customer-facing, non-booking pages): Home no longer queries the database at all — `StatsBar` renders its "Sports"/"Courts & Simulators" figures from hardcoded constants instead of `totalResources`/`sportCount` props, and `Home()` is a plain (non-async) Server Component with no `prisma` import (see 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry). Home page redesigned against a client-supplied reference image — design tokens reconciled (distinct page/card cream values, `accent-primary`), Choose Your Sport and Testimonials removed, Hero rebuilt full-bleed with a floating stats card overlapping the Hero/How It Works boundary, How It Works restyled, Gallery replaced by an asymmetric Facilities grid, CTA banner gained a repeating ring texture, Footer restyled — composed in `src/app/page.tsx` via per-section components under `src/components/home/` (no shared layout wrapper yet). Navbar is now `fixed` (was `sticky`) and transitions between a transparent/light-text state at the top of the page and its original solid/dark-text state once scrolled, with the transition itself later hardened so the background/logo genuinely interpolate rather than snap — see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entries (Navbar transparent-on-scroll + TwoSides background, then the scroll-transition/gradient/button-redesign pass). Navbar's wordmark text was later removed in favor of an emblem-only logo, and Hero was rebuilt again with a centered script "Winston" wordmark (`Mrs Saint Delafield`) over the real `hero-background.jpg` photo, replacing the earlier text-lockup/placeholder-photo version — see 2026-08-15 Completed Log entry. The site's font system was then fully replaced (see second 2026-08-15 entry): Lora is gone, body copy is now Manrope, the script wordmark is now Parisienne (not Mrs Saint Delafield), and Fraunces is loaded as a new `font-serif` utility (not yet used anywhere). Navbar lost its Sign In and Book Now buttons entirely (desktop and mobile) — the "Book Now" nav link is now the header's only booking CTA — and the logo was resized larger (44px/56px tall). Hero was rebuilt again to a `min-h-[85vh]` flex-centered layout with a repositioned chevron that scrolls to StatsBar via a new `id="stats-bar"` anchor (replacing the old `nextElementSibling` approach). Book Now's own visual reskin, and the News/Cafe & Bar/Membership/About pages themselves, are still pending. The StatsBar/How It Works copy+color pass (see 2026-08-15 Completed Log entry) has since landed — StatsBar now renders Fraunces numbers with no dividers and a symmetric overlap into both Hero and How It Works, and How It Works flipped to a dark `bg-brand-dark` section with bordered/tinted cards, Fraunces headings, and updated step copy. Navbar's link styling (uppercase, 0.35px tracking, weight 400) and CtaBanner's dot-pattern visibility/copy/button-casing were fixed in a 2026-08-17 pass. Navbar's Sign In/Book Now buttons (removed 2026-08-15) were reinstated on 2026-08-18 as "Book a Court" (→ `/book`) and "Become a Member" (→ `/membership`), desktop and mobile — see Completed Log. **A further Home layout pass landed 2026-08-20** (see Completed Log): Hero gained an "East Fairview's Private Sports Club" eyebrow above the wordmark; StatsBar dropped its floating-card overlap in favor of a full-width flat `bg-brand-dark` band sitting directly below Hero (same 4 stats, `neutral-100` text on the dark band); How It Works' bordered/tinted step cards were replaced with a connected 3-circle timeline (accent-primary-outlined Fraunces-numeral badges + a thin horizontal connector, desktop only); a new "Two Sides, One Winston" section (`src/components/home/TwoSides.tsx`) now sits between How It Works and Facilities — On the Court / At the Bar columns with two new hand-authored icons (`BullseyeIcon`, `CoffeeCupIcon` in `Icons.tsx`) and a small sunburst divider, CTAs to `/book` and `/cafe-bar` (the latter matching Navbar's own href verbatim); CtaBanner's ring/dot texture was replaced with a radiating sunburst-line texture (same gradient/copy/buttons, unchanged). Facilities was assumed already matching the target design at the time and was left byte-for-byte untouched — **this assumption turned out to be wrong** once an actual mock UI became available later the same day; see the second 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry below, which corrected Facilities (and every other section above) against the real reference via direct DOM/style diffing. Home's layout now matches that mock section-for-section (colors intentionally kept on our own brown/cream tokens rather than the mock's, per Arjay's explicit choice — see that entry). The one remaining staged follow-up on Home is a Facilities photography swap (real Café & Bar and Speakeasy Lounge photos, still pending from Arjay) — the copy/color/layout work itself is done. **A restraint pass landed later the same day** (see Completed Log): the Hero wordmark grew one size step at each breakpoint (`text-7xl md:text-9xl`), Navbar's "Book a Court"/"Become a Member" buttons now share identical `py-3.5` vertical padding on both desktop and mobile (fixing a height mismatch), and all 7 CTA button instances across Navbar/Hero/CtaBanner were dialed back from the gradient-fill/colored-shadow/lift/arrow-motion treatment (added earlier the same day) to a flat single-tone fill with a simple hover color-deepen and no motion — a deliberate calming of the button treatment toward the boutique/elegant brand direction. **This button treatment is now fully closed out** (see the following Completed Log entry): all 7 instances moved from `rounded-full` to a subtler `rounded-lg`, Navbar's two buttons shrunk from `py-3.5`/`px-9`-or-`px-5` to a more proportionate `py-2.5`/`px-6`-or-`px-5` (desktop and mobile alike, staying height-matched to each other), and "Become a Member" gained the same scroll-conditional light/dark color pattern the nav links already use so it's legible against the Hero photo when the header is transparent — Hero's and CtaBanner's button sizes were deliberately left untouched. **A mobile-responsiveness audit (audit-only, no fixes) has since been completed** across 320/375/414/639px widths — see the 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry — surfacing one blocking contrast bug (TwoSides' "Book a Court →" link is nearly unreadable against its own section's mobile vertical gradient) plus four polish-level findings; a fix pass against `docs/audits/2026-08-21-home-mobile-audit.md` is queued next.
- Admin panel: AdminUser email/password login via Auth.js (Credentials provider, JWT session, no adapter/DB session tables) live at /admin/login; `/admin` now uses a shared authenticated layout (route group `admin/(protected)/`) built as a bento-style shell — thin full-width topbar (title, initials avatar with email tooltip, Sign Out), a foldable left sidebar (`AdminSidebar`, 5 bento nav tiles that collapse to an icon rail with tooltips, active-tab highlighting via `usePathname()`), and page content wrapped in its own bento content card — see 2026-08-19 Completed Log entries (nav expansion, then sidebar/topbar restructure, then bookings list-page refinement, then filter-modal pass). Bookings list (`/admin/bookings`, 7-column layout with an independently-scrolling table body, filtering now modal-based — a single Filter button opens a modal with status + a working `startTime` date-range filter, both wired into the Prisma query and preserved across pagination; Export is a non-functional placeholder button only), booking detail (`/admin/bookings/[id]`, full detail + reschedule history), and admin-initiated reschedule (`PATCH /api/admin/bookings/[id]/reschedule`, confirmed bookings only) are now live. Memberships (`/admin/memberships` list + `/admin/memberships/[id]` detail) is also now live — see 2026-08-19 Completed Log entry — list/detail plus signed-URL government-ID image display, review actions (Approve/Reject) visibly present but intentionally not wired. Resources & Pricing (`/admin/resources`) is now tabbed (Courts / Simulators / Guest Fee, `ResourcesTabs.tsx`, client component); **Resource-level Add/Edit/Delete are now live** (`POST /api/admin/resources`, `PATCH`/`DELETE /api/admin/resources/[id]`, new `ResourceFormModal.tsx` — see 2026-08-19 Completed Log entry) — Pricing/Add-on/Guest-Fee buttons remain the inert CRUD-preview layer from the prior pass, still awaiting their own mutation slice. Courts/Simulators cards are now also individually foldable (collapsed by default, independent open/closed state per card, chevron toggle) — Guest Fee's card is not foldable and always shows its full content. **Pricing/Add-on/Guest-Fee rows are now live, edit-only** (`PATCH /api/admin/pricing-rules/[id]`, `PATCH /api/admin/add-on-pricing-rules/[id]`, `PATCH /api/admin/guest-fee-rule/[id]`, new `PriceEditModal.tsx` — see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry) — every previously-disabled pencil on the Pricing table, Add-on table, and Guest Fee card now opens a modal pre-filled with the current peso value(s) for whichever tier(s) actually have a row and saves on submit; create (the `+ Add` buttons) and delete for these three models remain disabled/deferred. Bulletin (`/admin/bulletin`) is now fully live too — see the latest 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry — a real card list sourced from the `Bulletin` table (or a "No bulletins yet." empty state), neutral admin styling, and Add/Edit/Delete are all wired (`POST`/`PATCH`/`DELETE /api/admin/bulletin[/[id]]`, new `BulletinFormModal.tsx`/`BulletinList.tsx`), including real image upload/replacement/cleanup against the `bulletin-images` bucket — the admin panel's Bulletin section now has full CRUD parity with Resources.
- PayMongo payment integration: Done — checkout session creation, webhook confirmation, checkout-session-expiry-on-hold-expiry, a manual (not automatic) hand-off from the wizard to PayMongo Checkout, and a polling confirmation page all live end-to-end.
- Membership application + approval flow (implementation): application submission done — public `/membership` page + `POST /api/membership-applications` create a pending `MembershipApplication` (customer resolved by email, 3 government ID images uploaded to a private Supabase Storage bucket). Admin review UI (`/admin/memberships` list + detail) is now live as a read-only frame — see 2026-08-19 Completed Log entry — but the actual approve/reject mutation (status change, `Membership` record creation on approval) is not yet wired; tier activation/payment also still pending.
- Bulletin/announcements (implementation): **Admin CRUD is now fully live** (see the latest 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry) — `/admin/bulletin` supports real Create/Edit/Delete against the `bulletins` table plus real image upload/replacement/cleanup against the public `bulletin-images` Storage bucket, closing out the admin side of this feature (parity with Resource-level CRUD elsewhere in the admin panel). Still open: `/book`'s `AnnouncementGate` and `/news`'s `NEWS_ITEMS` are still hardcoded arrays shaped to match the `Bulletin` model's fields (see 2026-08-17/2026-08-20 Completed Log entries) but not yet fetched from it — swapping both to live queries against this same table is the next and last remaining piece of this feature.
- Auth: Not started — method undecided (email/password vs. magic link)

- 2026-08-11 — CLAUDE.md updated: booking hold duration (10 min, `BOOKING_HOLD_MINUTES` env var) and hybrid expiry mechanism (expire-on-write primary, daily Vercel cron sweep secondary) documented as a new Architecture Decision; env var added to the Environment Variables table.
- 2026-08-11 — Pricing seeded: `PricingRule` (court rates as flat-hourly rows anchored at `durationMinutes=60`, simulator rates tiered by duration — non-member golf-sim 30-min tier intentionally has no row) and `GuestFeeRule` (₱150/hr per guest) added to `prisma/seed.ts`, following the existing upsert idempotency pattern. Prisma client singleton added at `src/lib/prisma.ts` (hot-reload-safe via `globalThis` caching). `POST /api/bookings` implemented: validates input, resolves `Customer` via look-up-or-create by email, derives member status server-side from an active (non-expired) `Membership`, validates duration against resource category (court = multiple of 60; simulator = must match a seeded tier), prices via `PricingRule` + `GuestFeeRule` (non-member court bookings only), and creates a `pending_payment` hold inside a transaction that first expires stale holds for the same resource/time range (expire-on-write) — a caught `booking_no_overlap` exclusion violation returns 409. `GET /api/cron/expire-bookings` implemented with `CRON_SECRET` bearer-token auth (401 if unset/mismatched) and a platform-wide stale-hold sweep; `vercel.json` schedules it daily (`0 0 * * *`, Hobby-plan cron cap). CLAUDE.md's API routes convention and `CRON_SECRET` env var row filled in.
- 2026-08-11 — `GET /api/resources` added (public, no auth): returns all `ResourceType` rows with nested active `Resource` rows and their `PricingRule` tiers grouped inline, plus the single `GuestFeeRule` amount at the top level, sized so the client can populate dropdowns and estimate price without extra joins. Booking form UI built: `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx` (client component, folder-per-feature under `src/components/`) fetches `/api/resources` on mount, drives a resource-type → resource → duration cascade (court types offer a fixed 60/120/180-min select; simulator types offer the union of seeded duration tiers for that type), shows the guest-count input only for court categories, and computes a live non-member-rate price estimate client-side (clearly labeled as a preview, never authoritative). Submits to the existing `POST /api/bookings` unchanged, branching on response status: 201 swaps in `BookingConfirmation.tsx` with the server-returned booking details, 409 shows the required "just booked by someone else" inline message, 400 surfaces the server's own `error` text, and other/network failures show a generic inline message — all three failure paths preserve form state. `src/app/book/page.tsx` renders the form; homepage (`src/app/page.tsx`) replaced with venue name, one-line description, and a single "Book Now" link to `/book` (no bulletin, no resource grid, per current scope). No new dependencies added; `prisma/schema.prisma`, `prisma/manual-sql/*`, `prisma/seed.ts`, `POST /api/bookings`, the cron route, and `vercel.json` were not touched. CLAUDE.md's Components and Error handling conventions filled in based on the pattern established here.
- 2026-08-11 — Booking flow restructured into an announcement gate + 5-step wizard, structure-only pass (no visual/branding polish, no new dependencies). `src/components/booking/AnnouncementGate.tsx` added: a static placeholder announcement card with a "Continue to Booking" button; `src/app/book/page.tsx` (now a client component) holds a `started` boolean toggling between the gate and the wizard, no route change. `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx` rewritten as the wizard orchestrator — all form state (resource type/resource/date-time/duration/guest count/name/phone/email/add-on checkboxes, plus `step`) lives here and is passed down as props, so Back/Continue navigation never resets a previously entered value; the existing resource-type → resource/duration cascade, price-estimate calc, and submit/response-branching logic (201/409/400/network) are reused verbatim, not rewritten. Split into `src/components/booking/steps/`: `StepIndicator.tsx` (5 numbered circles — SPORT/COURT/DATE & TIME/DETAILS/REVIEW), `SportStep.tsx` (all 5 real `ResourceType` cards with live resource counts, no images), `CourtStep.tsx`, `DateTimeStep.tsx`, `DetailsStep.tsx` (adds Ball Boy/Coaching checkboxes with a "Coming soon — not yet included in your booking" note), and `ReviewStep.tsx` (read-only summary of every prior step plus the reused price estimate; its "Confirm Booking" button fires the existing submit logic). Ball Boy/Coaching are UI-only — confirmed via a `fetch` patch on the actual outgoing `POST /api/bookings` body during browser verification that neither field is ever serialized, regardless of checkbox state. On 409/400/network failure the wizard stays on the Review step (not step 1) with all values intact, per existing error-handling convention. VERIFY performed in the connected browser: full tennis-court booking walked through all 5 steps end-to-end (201, confirmation shown with server values); Back → change guestCount → forward confirmed the update applied with nothing else reset; 409 (duplicate slot) and 400 (non-member golf-sim 30-min, no seeded tier) both reproduced from Review with the correct existing inline messages and state preserved; `GET /api/resources`/`POST /api/bookings` shapes, `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint, and RLS deny-all policies (16/16 tables) all confirmed unchanged via Supabase MCP; cron route re-verified 401 (no auth) / 200 (correct bearer); `package.json` diff empty. Test `Booking`/`Customer` rows created during verification were deleted via a one-off Prisma script (Supabase MCP is read-only) — baseline confirmed back at 5/9/21/1 resource-side rows, 0 bookings/customers.

- 2026-08-11 — DATE & TIME step replaced the native `datetime-local` input with a real calendar + availability grid, and business-hours enforcement (6:00 AM–10:00 PM PH) added server-side. `src/lib/booking-hold.ts` extracted from `POST /api/bookings` (`HOLD_MINUTES` + an `occupyingSlotCondition(now)` Prisma where-fragment for "confirmed, or a pending_payment hold that hasn't gone stale") so the new read-only availability endpoint and booking creation can never disagree on the hold window. `src/lib/business-hours.ts` added: `BUSINESS_OPEN_HOUR`/`BUSINESS_CLOSE_HOUR` constants, PH-offset (fixed UTC+8, no DST) minutes-of-day/date-string helpers, `isWithinBusinessHours`, and a PH-calendar-date → UTC-day-window helper. `GET /api/availability` (public, no auth) added: given `resourceId` + `date` (YYYY-MM-DD, PH calendar date), returns `{ busy: [{ start, end }] }` for that resource/day using the shared occupying-slot condition — no pricing or customer data. `POST /api/bookings` now 400s with `'Bookings must start and end between 6:00 AM and 10:00 PM'` on any out-of-hours submission, checked immediately after the existing duration validation and before customer look-up-or-create (fail fast). Two new hand-rolled (no new dependencies) client components: `src/components/booking/Calendar.tsx` (month-grid date picker, Prev disabled at the current PH month, no upper bound on Next, past days disabled) and `src/components/booking/TimeSlotGrid.tsx` (granularity 60min court / 30min golf-sim / 15min tennis-sim & pickleball-sim, filters out busy and already-past slots, `+08:00`-qualified ISO start times). `DateTimeStep.tsx` rewritten to render duration select → Calendar → TimeSlotGrid (grid only once a date is picked); `BookingForm.tsx` gained `selectedDate`/`busy`/`availabilityLoading`/`availabilityError` state, an availability-fetch effect keyed on `resourceId`+`selectedDate`, and a slot-reset effect keyed on `resourceId`/`selectedDate`/`durationMinutes` (mirrors the existing resource-type-change reset pattern) so a stale slot selection can't survive an invalidating change; `startTimeLocal` is now only ever set via the grid's `onSelectSlot`, already `+08:00`-qualified, so the existing submit body construction needed no change. SPORT/COURT/DETAILS/REVIEW steps, the exclusion constraint, RLS policies, and the cron route were not touched. VERIFY performed in the connected browser pane (this environment's browser tooling, not the Playwright MCP referenced in CLAUDE.md's MCP Servers list — no functional difference for these checks): hourly (tennis court) / 30-min (golf-sim, both 30-min and 90-min tiers) / 15-min (tennis-sim) grid granularity confirmed correct at the 6am–10pm boundary, including duration-dependent last-start-time shift (e.g. golf-sim 90-min duration's last start is 8:30 PM); a real Tennis Court booking's overlapping 2:00 PM slot correctly disabled in the grid on reload, non-overlapping slots unaffected; a fabricated 15-minutes-stale `pending_payment` row correctly excluded from `GET /api/availability`'s `busy` array (regression check on the `booking-hold.ts` extraction); today's past-time slots correctly hidden (verified against actual current PH time) while future dates show the full range; calendar Prev/Next navigation confirmed (Prev disabled only at the current PH month, re-verified after navigating 3 months forward and back); full 5-step happy path completed end to end (201, confirmation screen's PH start/end time matched the Review step exactly, no timezone shift); direct `POST /api/bookings` with an out-of-hours `startTime` (5:00 AM and 10:30 PM PH) both returned 400 with the business-hours message, confirming the UI can't be bypassed. - 2026-08-11 — `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` refined: the selection highlight now spans the whole occupied range (clicked start through start+duration) as one solid block, instead of only the exact clicked slot — visible whenever a booking's duration spans more than one of the grid's own granularity increments (e.g. a 30-min tennis-sim booking on the 15-min grid, or a 90-min golf-sim booking on the 30-min grid). `Slot` now carries its already-computed `startDate`; `selectedRange` (`{ start, end }` derived from `selectedSlot` + `durationMinutes`) is computed once before the slot map, and each slot's `isInSelectedRange` replaces the old exact-`startIso`-match check. Fixed a related className-precedence bug: a slot can now be individually `disabled` (its own duration window happens to overlap a later busy range) while still falling inside the current selection's already-validated span — the disabled-grey and selected-highlight class strings were previously appended independently (order in the template string doesn't guarantee visual precedence in generated Tailwind CSS), so restructured to a single three-way conditional where the highlight takes priority, with a separately-appended `cursor-not-allowed` fragment so an in-range-but-disabled slot still shows a not-allowed cursor (its `disabled` attribute — and therefore non-clickability — is untouched). Purely a visual change: granularity, past/busy filtering, the `disabled` attribute, and `onSelectSlot` wiring are all unchanged; only `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` was touched. VERIFY in the browser pane: single-slot selections (60-min court on the 60-min grid) render identically to before (exactly one highlighted button); a 30-min tennis-sim selection on the 15-min grid highlights both occupied 15-min slots as one block; a 90-min golf-sim selection on the 30-min grid highlights exactly 3 consecutive slots; changing duration after selecting still clears the highlight (existing reset effect, untouched); clicking a different unoccupied slot moves the highlight entirely; a fabricated booking made two slots within an active multi-slot selection individually `disabled: true` while the selected start itself stayed enabled — both disabled-in-range slots rendered with the highlight style (not grey) and remained non-clickable (a click on them left the selection unchanged), while a `disabled` slot outside the selection kept the original grey styling; full happy path (golf-sim, 90-min duration, multi-slot selection) completed end to end with 201, confirmation showing the actually-clicked start time. Regression-confirmed: `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint present; cron route still 401/200; `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff empty. Cleanup via one-off Prisma scripts removed the two test bookings and their `Customer` rows — baseline (5/9/21/1, 0 bookings/customers) confirmed restored.
  - **Correction (same day):** the range-highlight comparison above was exclusive of the end boundary (`slot.startDate < selectedRange.end`), so the grid slot marking where the booking actually ends never lit up — a 30-min booking on its matching-granularity grid still only highlighted 1 slot, not the full 2-slot span. Changed to inclusive (`<=`) so the slot at the booking's end time now highlights too — a 30-min golf-sim booking now highlights 2 slots (was 1), a 60-min court booking now highlights 2 slots (was 1), a 90-min golf-sim booking now highlights 4 slots (was 3). Re-verified the disabled/in-range precedence fix still holds at this now-included end-boundary slot (a fabricated booking overlapping only the end-boundary slot's own duration window rendered it highlighted, not grey, and non-clickable) and that a full happy-path booking still submits the correct actually-clicked `startTime`. Only `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` touched; `package.json` diff empty.

- 2026-08-11 — `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` now always renders the full 6:00 AM–10:00 PM range regardless of duration, instead of the grid's loop previously stopping early once a slot's own duration wouldn't fit before closing (e.g. a 120-min court booking's grid used to stop at 8:00 PM, with 9:00 PM never appearing). The loop condition changed from `m + durationMinutes <= closeMinutes` to `m < closeMinutes`; a new `Slot.exceedsClosing` field marks any slot whose selected duration would run past 10:00 PM. These slots stay clickable (not HTML-`disabled`) and render in a subtle amber/warning style — but rank below the existing disabled-grey style in visual priority, so a slot that's genuinely busy or in the past still shows as disabled even if it would also exceed closing. Clicking an `exceedsClosing` slot no longer silently does nothing; it shows a dismissible toast (auto-clears after 4s) naming the selected duration and closing time, and does not change the current selection. Granularity, busy-overlap detection, past-time detection, the range-highlight feature, and click-to-select for genuinely valid slots are all unchanged. Only `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` touched; no new dependencies. VERIFY performed in the browser pane: Tennis Court/120-min grid now shows every hourly slot 6:00 AM–9:00 PM (previously stopped at 8:00 PM), with 9:00 PM in the warning style and 8:00 PM and earlier normal; clicking 9:00 PM showed the toast with no selection change, and it auto-dismissed after ~4s; clicking a normal slot (6:00 AM) produced ordinary selection/highlight with no toast; Golf Sim/90-min grid confirmed the last-fitting slot (8:30 PM, ends exactly 10:00 PM) renders normal while 9:00 PM/9:30 PM show the warning and trigger the toast on click; a real booking made to overlap a late slot (Golf Sim Bay 1, Aug 20, 9:00–10:00 PM) correctly rendered 8:30 PM/9:00 PM/9:30 PM as disabled/grey (not warning) despite also exceeding closing at the 90-min duration, confirming disabled takes priority; today's already-past slots still rendered disabled/grey exactly as before; the existing range-highlight (start-through-end shading) still worked correctly on a valid selection; a full happy-path booking (the same Golf Sim booking used for the busy-slot regression check) completed end-to-end with 201. `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff empty; no file other than `TimeSlotGrid.tsx` touched. Test `Booking`/`Customer` row created for verification was deleted via a one-off Prisma script — baseline (5 ResourceType / 9 Resource / 21 PricingRule / 1 GuestFeeRule, 0 bookings/customers) confirmed restored.
  - **Correction (same day):** the previous fix's loop bound (`m < closeMinutes`) still stopped one increment short of closing itself, so a slot starting exactly at 10:00 PM was never generated — invisible for any duration equal to the resource's own grid granularity (e.g. Tennis Court's 60-min booking on its 60-min grid still topped out at 9:00 PM, Tennis/Pickleball Sim's 15-min booking on the 15-min grid still topped out at 9:45 PM), since those cases never had a slot beyond the existing max for the fix to reveal. - 2026-08-12 — Add-on backend wiring, no frontend changes. `prisma/seed.ts`: `AddOnService` seeded (2 rows: `ball_boy` "Ball Boy", `coaching_fee` "Coaching", upsert-by-slug) and `AddOnPricingRule` seeded (16 rows: Ball Boy court-only both tiers at ₱150, Coaching courts pax-1/pax-2 both tiers, Coaching simulators member-only for Tennis/Pickleball Sim at ₱800 and both tiers for Golf Sim at ₱1,000 — non-member Tennis Sim/Pickleball Sim coaching intentionally has no row, same "no row = not offered" precedent as the existing non-member golf-sim 30-min `PricingRule`). Hit a Prisma limitation along the way: its compound-unique input type (and the runtime validator behind `.upsert()`/`.findUnique()`) rejects `null` for a nullable field in a composite key, even though the DB column and the actual unique constraint allow it — worked around by using `findFirst` + conditional `create`/`update` in the seed loop (same pattern already used for `Resource` seeding) instead of `.upsert()`, and `findFirst` instead of `.findUnique()` in the two lookups added to `POST /api/bookings`. `GET /api/resources` extended with an `addOnPricing` array per `ResourceType` (joined `AddOnPricingRule`+`AddOnService`, both rate tiers, no server-side tier filtering — same convention as the existing `pricing` array). `POST /api/bookings` extended: `ballBoy`/`coaching`/`coachingPaxCount` accepted as optional fields (default `false`/absent), validated (ball boy 400s on non-court resources; court coaching requires `coachingPaxCount` of exactly 1 or 2; simulator/non-court coaching ignores any `coachingPaxCount` sent), priced by looking up the matching `AddOnPricingRule` per resource type + rate tier + pax (400 if no row — this is what naturally rejects non-member Tennis Sim/Pickleball Sim coaching), and persisted as one `BookingAddOn` row per add-on inside the existing transaction (price snapshotted onto `amountCentavos`, per the schema's own doc comment). Response gained `addOns: [{ service, paxCount, amountCentavos }]` and a top-level `addOnsTotalCentavos` — `totalAmountCentavos` is untouched, still scoped to court/sim rate + guest fee only. `prisma/schema.prisma` and all frontend files (`BookingForm.tsx`, `AddOnsStep.tsx`, etc.) were not touched — the UI still never sends these fields, confirmed by a real end-to-end browser booking (Tennis Court, Ball Boy/Coaching left unchecked) whose actual `POST /api/bookings` request produced `addOns: [], addOnsTotalCentavos: 0` in the response, same as before this prompt. VERIFY performed via Supabase MCP + direct `POST`/`GET` calls against the local dev server (already running, hot-reloaded the route changes) plus one browser pass: seed row counts confirmed exactly 2 `AddOnService` / 16 `AddOnPricingRule`, with the two non-member sim-coaching combinations confirmed absent by direct row dump; re-running `npm run db:seed` left all row counts unchanged (idempotent); `GET /api/resources` spot-checked for Tennis Court (ball_boy + coaching pax 1/2 both tiers) and Tennis Sim (coaching, member only); direct `POST /api/bookings` calls covered all 7 scenarios in the originating prompt (Tennis Court ballBoy+coaching pax1 → `addOnsTotalCentavos: 95000`; pax2 → `135000`; Golf Sim coaching no pax → `100000`, `paxCount: null`; Tennis Sim coaching → 400; Golf Sim ballBoy → 400; Tennis Court coaching without pax → 400; no add-on fields at all → `addOns: [], addOnsTotalCentavos: 0`), each matching the prompt's expected values exactly; the three successful bookings' `BookingAddOn` rows confirmed via Supabase MCP to have the correct snapshotted `amountCentavos`. Regression-confirmed via Supabase MCP: `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint present; RLS enabled on 16/16 tables with 32 deny-all policies unchanged. `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff empty; `git status` confirmed only the three in-scope files (`prisma/seed.ts`, `src/app/api/bookings/route.ts`, `src/app/api/resources/route.ts`) touched. Five test `Booking`/`BookingAddOn` rows and two test `Customer` rows created during verification (one from the direct-`POST` scenarios, one from the browser pass) deleted via a one-off Prisma script run from inside the project directory (Supabase MCP is read-only) — baseline confirmed restored, now including the new seed data: 5 `ResourceType` / 9 `Resource` / 21 `PricingRule` / 1 `GuestFeeRule` / 2 `AddOnService` / 16 `AddOnPricingRule`, 0 bookings/customers/bookingAddOns.

Coaching is now gated on `coachingPricing.available` (true only when at least one non-member `coaching_fee` row exists for the resource type) instead of rendering unconditionally, which is what hides it for Tennis Sim/Pickleball Sim; - 2026-08-12 — PayMongo Checkout Session backend implemented (Payment Intents + client-side Elements deliberately not used — hosted Checkout Sessions instead, now documented as a new CLAUDE.md Architecture Decision). Backend-only; no file under `src/components/booking/` touched. `prisma/schema.prisma`: `Payment` gained `paymongoCheckoutSessionId String? @unique @map("paymongo_checkout_session_id")`, placed above the existing `paymongoPaymentIntentId`. Applied as an isolated migration (`20260812055040_add_paymongo_checkout_session_id_to_payments`) — `prisma migrate dev` refused to run in this non-interactive environment even with `--create-only`, so the migration SQL was hand-written (plain `ALTER TABLE`/`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX`, matching Prisma's own generated form for this column shape) and applied via `prisma migrate deploy`, then `prisma generate` re-run — no schema drift, confirmed via `prisma migrate deploy`'s own history check and a direct Supabase MCP column/index query. `src/lib/paymongo.ts` added: `createPaymongoCheckoutSession`, `expirePaymongoCheckoutSession` (best-effort, internally catches, never throws), `verifyPaymongoWebhookSignature` (HMAC-SHA256 over `t.rawBody`, timing-safe comparison against `te`), plus one function beyond the originating prompt's explicit export list — `retrievePaymongoCheckoutSession` — added to support `POST /api/checkout`'s idempotent-retry path (Part 4 step 3 needs a Retrieve Checkout Session call; adding it here reuses the module's private Basic-auth header builder instead of duplicating that logic at the route level, consistent with the module's own "extract shared logic once" rationale). `POST /api/checkout` (new) accepts `{ bookingId }`, resolves the booking with its resource/customer/add-ons, is idempotent on retry (returns the existing session's current `checkout_url` without creating a second `Payment` row), 409s if the hold has expired or the booking isn't payable, builds one line item for the base booking plus one per add-on (pax count included in the name when applicable), creates the PayMongo session and a `Payment` row (`status: pending`), and returns `{ checkoutUrl }`. `POST /api/webhooks/paymongo` (new) reads the raw body before any parsing, verifies `Paymongo-Signature`, ignores non-`checkout_session.payment.paid` events (200, no-op), extracts `bookingId` from `metadata`, is idempotent (no-op if already `paid`), and in one transaction marks `Payment` paid (with `paidAt`/`paymongoPaymentIntentId`) and flips `Booking` to `confirmed` only if it's still `pending_payment` — otherwise logs a clearly-labeled manual-review error instead of silently overwriting a `cancelled` booking. `POST /api/bookings` and `GET /api/cron/expire-bookings` both restructured identically: the stale-hold cleanup now `findMany`s first (capturing linked `Payment.paymongoCheckoutSessionId` where still `pending`) before `updateMany`-cancelling the bookings and `updateMany`-failing their payments inside the transaction; after the transaction commits, `expirePaymongoCheckoutSession` is called per captured session id outside the transaction so a slow/failing PayMongo call can never hold the DB transaction open. Neither route's external request/response shape changed. CLAUDE.md's Tech Stack table corrected to "Next.js 16 (App Router) + TypeScript" (was unversioned; confirmed 16.3.0 via `package.json` and dev server output) — no other row touched.
  - **Deviation flagged**: live-tested against PayMongo's actual Checkout Sessions API and found it rejects requests with `payment_method_types` omitted (`"Parameter payment_method_types is required"`), contradicting the originating prompt's instruction to leave it unset. - 2026-08-12 — Wizard wired to real PayMongo Checkout and a live polling confirmation page — the booking + payment flow is now complete end-to-end. CLAUDE.md's Tech Stack Payments row corrected to "PayMongo (Checkout Sessions API)" and the `paymongo-integration` skill's Claude Code Skills bullet reworded to describe the Checkout Session flow instead of the stale "Payment Intent flow" wording (both doc-drift only, no code). `GET /api/bookings/[id]` added (new, public/no-auth, same trust tier as `GET /api/resources`): `await params` per Next.js 16's promise-based dynamic route params; returns booking details in a shape reusing `POST /api/bookings`' field names where the concept already existed (`id`/`status`/`startTime`/`endTime`/`totalAmountCentavos`/`addOns`/`addOnsTotalCentavos`) plus new fields this page needs (`resource: { typeName, label }`, `guestCount`, `customer: { name }` — email/phone deliberately omitted from this public response, `payment: { status, amountCentavos, paidAt } | null`); 404s on unknown id. `src/app/book/confirmation/page.tsx` (new, server component) wraps `ConfirmationContent.tsx` (new, client component, co-located in the same route folder since it's route-specific rather than a reusable feature component) in `<Suspense>` per Next.js's `useSearchParams()` requirement. `ConfirmationContent` reads `bookingId` from the query string; shows a no-fetch "no booking reference" state if absent; fetches `GET /api/bookings/{id}` on mount; 404 renders a distinct "couldn't find that booking" state; `pending_payment` polls the same endpoint every 2s (via `setTimeout` chaining, not `setInterval`, so a slow response can't overlap the next tick) up to 15 attempts (~30s) then stops and shows a "taking longer than expected" state with a manual "Check Again" button (one more fetch, resumes the normal branching — not a new auto-poll loop); a transient fetch error during polling doesn't hard-stop the loop, it just counts toward the same 15-attempt budget and retries; `confirmed` renders the reused `BookingConfirmation` component; `cancelled` renders a distinct state (different from loading and success) surfacing the booking id, since this is the webhook handler's rare manual-review edge case and a customer who actually paid needs to know to contact support, not see a generic error. `BookingConfirmation.tsx` adapted (its only consumer was the inline post-201 render this prompt removes, so this was a clean break, not a compat shim): now takes a single `booking` prop shaped like the `GET /api/bookings/[id]` response (exported as `BookingDetail`, replacing the old `BookingSuccess` type keyed to the `POST /api/bookings` response) instead of `booking`+`resourceLabel`+`resourceTypeName` separately; heading changed from "Booking held" to "Booking confirmed" and the closing note now confirms payment instead of claiming a team follow-up, since this component only ever renders once `status === 'confirmed'`; a guest-count line was added (only when `guestCount > 0`) since the new response shape carries it and it's consistent with `ReviewStep`'s existing convention. `BookingForm.tsx`: removed the inline `BookingConfirmation` render entirely — a 201 from `POST /api/bookings` no longer shows a confirmation screen itself, it stores the id in new `bookingId` state and immediately calls a new `startCheckout(id)` helper (`POST /api/checkout`, then `window.location.href = checkoutUrl` on success — a full browser navigation, not `router.push`, since the target is PayMongo's domain); `submitting`/`submitError` still cover exactly the `POST /api/bookings` phase and its existing 409/400/network branches, completely unchanged; new `checkingOut`/`checkoutError` state covers the `POST /api/checkout` phase; `handleRetryCheckout` re-calls `startCheckout` with the already-stored `bookingId` only (confirmed via source read — it never touches `POST /api/bookings`); `handleStartOver` resets `step` to 1 and every piece of wizard state including `bookingId` back to its initial default. `ReviewStep.tsx` gained `checkingOut`/`checkoutError`/`onRetryCheckout`/`onStartOver` props (button/loading-state markup already lived here, confirmed by reading both files first, so the new states were wired here rather than in `BookingForm.tsx` — steps still hold no state of their own, per the existing wizard convention): when `checkoutError` is set, the normal Back/Confirm row is replaced by the error text plus Start Over/Try Again buttons (Try Again labeled "Redirecting to payment…" and disabled while `checkingOut`); otherwise the existing Back/Confirm row is shown with the Confirm button's label now three-way (`submitting` → "Creating your booking…", `checkingOut` → "Redirecting to payment…", else "Confirm Booking") and both Back and Confirm disabled during either phase. `AnnouncementGate.tsx`, `SportStep.tsx`, `CourtStep.tsx`, `DateTimeStep.tsx`, `DetailsStep.tsx`, `AddOnsStep.tsx`, `StepIndicator.tsx`, `TimeSlotGrid.tsx`, `Calendar.tsx`, every Prisma/schema/migration file, and `POST /api/bookings`/`POST /api/checkout`/`POST /api/webhooks/paymongo`/`GET /api/resources`/`GET /api/availability`/the cron route were not touched. No new dependencies.
  - **Finding, not fixed here (outside this prompt's file scope and outside code entirely)**: live-tested the real redirect back from PayMongo after a successful test payment and it 404'd on `checkout.paymongo.com` instead of landing on `/book/confirmation`. Root cause confirmed via the PayMongo API and a byte-level inspection of `.env.local`: `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=""` — an empty string, not a real URL — so the pre-existing (unchanged this prompt) `POST /api/checkout` route's `` `${appUrl}/book/confirmation?...` `` / `` `${appUrl}/book` `` template strings are sending PayMongo bare relative paths (`/book/confirmation?...`, `/book`) instead of absolute URLs, and PayMongo stores/redirects to exactly what it's given. This is a `.env.local` value problem, not a code defect — `.env.local` isn't in this prompt's file scope and env values are Arjay's to set, so it wasn't edited here. **Action needed before the redirect will work end-to-end: set `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL="http://localhost:3000"` (or the current ngrok URL, if customers should land through the tunnel) in `.env.local` and restart the dev server** (Next.js only reads `.env.local` at server boot, so an already-running process won't pick up a value added/fixed after it started). Everything downstream of that redirect was verified working correctly by navigating directly to `/book/confirmation?bookingId=...` instead (see VERIFY below) — this is purely a broken hop between PayMongo and the app, not a problem with the confirmation page, the polling logic, or `GET /api/bookings/[id]`.

- 2026-08-12 — `Booking.customerId` made nullable and booking creation split into a two-phase flow: the hold (with a real Booking Reference) is created before the customer is known, and customer resolution + member-aware final pricing happens in a separate step once Name/Phone/Email are collected on the payment page — CLAUDE.md's "Customer & auth model" decision is amended to document this as a deliberate deviation, with a new "Two-phase booking pricing" bullet added alongside it.
  - **Part 1 (migration)**: `customerId` changed to `String?` / optional relation in `prisma/schema.prisma`. Following the established `paymongo_checkout_session_id` precedent, `prisma migrate dev` was not used (non-interactive environment); the migration SQL was hand-written (`ALTER TABLE "bookings" ALTER COLUMN "customer_id" DROP NOT NULL;`, matching Prisma's own generated form for this change) at `prisma/migrations/20260812120000_make_booking_customer_id_nullable/`, applied via `prisma migrate deploy`, then `prisma generate` re-run. The running `next dev` process (started outside this session) held a Windows file lock on the generated query-engine binary, causing an `EPERM` on the first `prisma generate` attempt — the dev server processes were stopped (Playwright MCP's own node processes were left untouched) to release the lock, `prisma generate` was re-run successfully, and the dev server was restarted afterward via the preview tool for VERIFY.
  - **Parts 2–3 (shared logic extraction)**: `src/lib/booking-pricing.ts` added — the exact price-computation block from the old `POST /api/bookings` (court-duration validation, base `PricingRule` lookup, guest-fee-for-non-member-courts, Ball Boy/Coaching `AddOnPricingRule` lookups) extracted verbatim into an exported `priceBooking()` function, parameterized by `isMember: boolean` instead of resolving membership itself; returns either the same `{ totalAmountCentavos, addOns, addOnsTotalCentavos }` shape as before or a structured `{ error, status }`. `src/lib/customer-resolution.ts` added — the exact look-up-or-create-`Customer`-by-email-then-check-active-`Membership` block extracted verbatim into an exported `resolveCustomer()` function returning `{ customer, isMember }`.
  - **Part 4 (`POST /api/bookings`)**: `customer`/name/phone/email removed entirely from the request contract (not optional — absent from the type and from validation). No `Customer` look-up-or-create happens in this route anymore; pricing is computed via `priceBooking(..., isMember: false)` and the booking is created with `customerId: null`. Duration validation, business-hours enforcement, expire-on-write, and exclusion-constraint handling (409 on `booking_no_overlap`) are all unchanged in logic and order — duration validation still runs explicitly in the route before the business-hours check (unchanged ordering), and is re-run redundantly inside `priceBooking` itself (harmless — needed there for the `PATCH` re-pricing case in Part 5, since both routes share the one function). Response shape (`id`/`totalAmountCentavos`/`addOns`/`addOnsTotalCentavos`/etc.) unchanged.
  - **Part 5 (new `PATCH /api/bookings/[id]`)**: added alongside the existing `GET` in the same file. Accepts `{ name, phone, email }` (400 if any is missing/empty); 404 if the booking doesn't exist; 409 — mirroring `POST /api/checkout`'s exact condition and message ("Booking hold has expired or booking is no longer payable") — if the booking isn't `pending_payment` *or* its hold has aged past `HOLD_MINUTES` (not status alone, since a stale-but-still-`pending_payment` hold needs this check to actually catch it, matching the existing hybrid expire-on-write/cron precedent rather than assuming some other process already flipped its status). Resolves the customer via `resolveCustomer()`, derives `ballBoy`/`coaching`/`coachingPaxCount` from the booking's existing `BookingAddOn` rows (service slug + `addOnPricingRule.paxCount`), and re-prices via the same `priceBooking()` with the now-known `isMember`. In one transaction: sets `Booking.customerId`, updates `Booking.totalAmountCentavos` only if it changed, and updates each existing `BookingAddOn.amountCentavos` in place (matched back to the repriced result by service slug) only if it changed — rows are never deleted/recreated, and `addOnPricingRuleId` is deliberately left pointing at whichever rule was set at creation time, since `amountCentavos` is the schema's own documented source of truth for what was actually charged, not the FK. Returns `{ totalAmountCentavos, addOnsTotalCentavos, isMember }`.
  - **Part 6 (`GET /api/bookings/[id]`)**: `customer` field is now null-safe (`booking.customer ? { name } : null`), covering the window between hold creation and customer attachment. No other field or behavior changed.
  - **Necessary out-of-explicit-scope fix**: `POST /api/checkout` (marked "no changes to that route itself" in this prompt's scope) failed to type-check once `Booking.customer` became nullable, since it reads `booking.customer.name/email/phone` when building the PayMongo billing payload. Added a minimal defensive check — `if (!booking.customer) return 409 'Booking is not ready for checkout'` — placed after the existing hold-age/status 409 check, matching the file's own existing defensive-error convention (e.g. its "payment exists with no checkout session id" 502 case). This doesn't change the route's `{ bookingId }` in / `{ checkoutUrl }` out contract or its behavior for any legitimate call (a booking always has a customer attached by the time checkout is reachable in the new flow) — it only makes an now-representable-but-practically-impossible null case handled instead of a TypeScript compile error.
  - **Parts 7–9 (frontend)**: `ReviewStep.tsx` — "Confirm Booking" now calls a new `onConfirmBooking` prop (was a pure `onProceedToPayment` state flip) and the component gained `submitting`/`submitError` props/markup ("Creating your booking…" while submitting, inline error + re-enabled button on failure), mirroring every other API-backed step's existing convention. `PaymentStep.tsx` reworked: `bookingId` is now always non-null when rendered (always shows "Booking Reference: {bookingId}", not conditional); gained `attachingCustomer`/`attachError`/`customerAttached`/`priceUpdate`/`onPayNow`/`onStartOver` props, lost `onBack` and its Back button entirely; Name/Phone/Email fields become `disabled` once `customerAttached`; a price-change notice ("Your final price is ₱X (was ₱Y for non-members) — member rate applied.") renders above the button when `priceUpdate` is set, using the same `formatCentavos` helper as every other price display in the app; the button cycles through "Pay Now" → "Confirming your details…" (disabled) → either straight to "Redirecting to payment…" (unchanged total) or "Continue to Payment" (changed total, requires the extra click) → "Redirecting to payment…"; Start Over is unconditionally visible (not gated on an error state, since it's now the only way to leave this page). `BookingForm.tsx`: `handleConfirmBooking` (new) does the `POST /api/bookings` call (same body construction as before, minus name/phone/email) and on 201 stores `bookingId` + the server-authoritative `createdTotalCentavos` (`totalAmountCentavos + addOnsTotalCentavos` from the response, not the client-side estimate memo) before revealing the Payment page; `handlePayNow` (new) calls `PATCH /api/bookings/[id]` on first invocation (`!customerAttached`), then either auto-continues into the existing `startCheckout` if the total didn't change or sets `priceUpdate` and stops if it did — a second `onPayNow` invocation (`customerAttached` already true, whether from a price-change confirmation click or a checkout-session-only retry) skips straight to `startCheckout` and never calls `PATCH` again for the same booking; `handleStartOver` extended to reset every new field (`createdTotalCentavos`/`attachingCustomer`/`attachError`/`customerAttached`/`priceUpdate`) alongside the existing reset scope; the `onBack` wiring to `PaymentStep` was removed. `AnnouncementGate.tsx`, `SportStep.tsx`, `CourtStep.tsx`, `DateTimeStep.tsx`, `TimeSlotGrid.tsx`, `Calendar.tsx`, `AddOnsStep.tsx`, `StepIndicator.tsx`, `GET /api/resources`, `GET /api/availability`, the cron route, `POST /api/webhooks/paymongo`, `src/lib/paymongo.ts`, and `src/lib/business-hours.ts` were not touched. - 2026-08-12 — Critical webhook bugfix: real PayMongo `payment.paid` events were being silently no-op'd because `src/app/api/webhooks/paymongo/route.ts` matched on event type `checkout_session.payment.paid`, which PayMongo never actually sends — real deliveries send `payment.paid`. This was discovered via a genuine ngrok-captured webhook delivery for an actually-paid GCash test transaction (not a self-signed test payload), whose event silently 200'd as a no-op instead of confirming its booking, leaving booking `cmsq31lm30001w4m8b045hv2f` stranded `pending_payment` despite being genuinely paid on PayMongo's side. Fixed the event-type check (`data.attributes.type === 'payment.paid'`) and every downstream field-extraction path against the real payload shape, which nests the payment object one level deeper than previously assumed — `bookingId`/`status`/`payment_intent_id`/`paid_at` all live under `data.attributes.data.attributes`, not `data.attributes.data` directly. Also fixed `Payment.paidAt`, which was previously stamped with the webhook-processing server time instead of the payload's actual `paid_at` (Unix seconds, now converted via `new Date(paid_at * 1000)`). Signature verification itself (HMAC-SHA256 + `Paymongo-Signature` parsing) was already correct and untouched — only the event-type match and field paths below it were wrong. Only `src/app/api/webhooks/paymongo/route.ts` was code-touched; booking creation, pricing, checkout-session creation, frontend, and schema were not. **Flagging explicitly: every prior "webhook confirmed working" VERIFY entry in this log (see the 2026-08-12 line-items/payment-methods entry above, and earlier PayMongo-integration entries) was validated only against self-signed payloads built to match this same wrong `checkout_session.payment.paid` assumption — none were a real PayMongo delivery. - 2026-08-13 — Home page polish pass: reveal-on-scroll, CTA banner decorative blobs, image crop/tint variety, and hover micro-interactions — no new dependencies, no backend/schema/booking-flow changes. `src/components/ui/Reveal.tsx` (new): a `'use client'` `IntersectionObserver`-based wrapper (threshold 0.15, `-10%` bottom rootMargin) toggling `opacity-0 translate-y-4` → `opacity-100 translate-y-0` over 500ms ease-out, unobserving after first reveal so it never re-triggers on repeat scroll past the same element; accepts an optional `delayMs` for staggering; renders immediately visible with no observer at all when `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` is set. Applied with `delayMs={index * 100}` staggering to the 5 sport cards in `ChooseYourSport.tsx`, the 6 gallery tiles in `Gallery.tsx`, and the 3 testimonial cards in `Testimonials.tsx`; each of those three plus `HowItWorks.tsx` also got a single non-staggered `<Reveal>` around its heading block. `CtaBanner.tsx`: section wrapper gained `relative overflow-hidden`; two `pointer-events-none` blurred circles (`bg-accent`/`bg-brand-mid`, 20% opacity, `rounded-full blur-3xl`, ~320px) positioned bleeding off the top-right and bottom-left corners, sitting behind the text via `relative z-10` on the content wrapper. Image variety: `ChooseYourSport.tsx` and `Gallery.tsx` each gained module-level `objectPositions`/`tintColors` rotation arrays (cycled by index) applied as inline `style={{ objectPosition }}` plus a low-opacity (~10%) absolute tint overlay div per card/tile — `tintColors` is a literal array of full Tailwind class names (`'bg-brand-dark'`/`'bg-accent'`/`'bg-brand-mid'`), not a template-string-interpolated class, since Tailwind's compiler can't detect dynamically-built class names; `Hero.tsx` was left untouched (still full-quality, no tint, no crop change), confirmed via source diff. Micro-interactions: primary buttons (Hero's "Book a Court", CtaBanner's matching pair) changed from `hover:opacity-90`/plain to `transition-all duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-brand-mid`; outline buttons ("Explore the Cafe" in both places) and the My Account pill (desktop + mobile) gained `transition-all duration-200` alongside their existing `hover:bg-accent-light`; sport cards, gallery tiles, and testimonial cards all gained `transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-hero`, with gallery tiles additionally getting `group-hover:scale-105` on the image itself (contained by the tile's existing `overflow-hidden`, layered on top of the pre-existing dark-overlay-plus-"+"-icon hover effect rather than replacing it); Navbar nav links gained a `group`-based hover-in underline (`scale-x-0 group-hover:scale-x-100`, same accent color as the existing active-state underline, `transition-transform duration-200`) for inactive links, and the logo link gained `hover:scale-105 transition-transform`. VERIFY performed against the actual running dev server (found on port 3001 after discovering port 3000 is occupied by an unrelated pre-existing static-mock process — same coincidental collision flagged in the prior Home-page entry, not this prompt's doing) via DOM/computed-style inspection and `git status`, since this session's Browser pane never entered a composited/displayed state (`computer{action:"screenshot"}` and `computer{action:"scroll"}` both failed with "the Browser pane is not displayed, so the page is not compositing frames" — a session/tooling limitation, not a code issue). **Not visually confirmed this pass: actual on-screen fade/slide-in during a real scroll, real hover states, and `prefers-reduced-motion` emulation — Arjay should do a quick visual pass before treating this as fully signed off.** What was confirmed: initial computed style of every `Reveal`-wrapped block (ChooseYourSport/HowItWorks/Gallery/Testimonials headings and all 5/6/3 cards) reads `opacity: 0` / `translate: 0px 16px` with `transition-duration: 0.5s` and `ease-out` timing before any scroll, matching the spec; a freshly-created `IntersectionObserver` on the same element never fired within 2s even though the element's `getBoundingClientRect()` placed it well inside the 720px-tall viewport after a programmatic `scrollTo` — consistent with IntersectionObserver being suspended on a non-composited/non-visible tab in Chromium, not a bug in `Reveal.tsx` (its observer setup, threshold, rootMargin, and one-time `unobserve` follow the standard IntersectionObserver contract). CtaBanner: confirmed via DOM query that the section has `relative overflow-hidden` and exactly two `pointer-events-none` `blur-3xl` divs (`bg-accent`/`bg-brand-mid`, both `opacity-20`) positioned top-right/bottom-left. Image variety: confirmed via DOM query that all 5 ChooseYourSport cards and all 6 Gallery tiles have distinct `object-position` inline styles cycling through the 6-value array, each with a cycling low-opacity tint div (`bg-brand-dark`/`bg-accent`/`bg-brand-mid`); Hero's image confirmed to carry no tint div and no `object-position` override (`object-cover` only), unchanged. Hover classes confirmed present via static className inspection (not a live `:hover` trigger): Hero/CtaBanner primary buttons carry `hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-brand-mid`, outline buttons carry `hover:bg-accent-light`/`hover:bg-white/10`, My Account (desktop + mobile) carries `hover:bg-accent-light`, sport/gallery/testimonial cards carry `hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-hero`, gallery images additionally carry `group-hover:scale-105`, inactive nav links render the new `scale-x-0 group-hover:scale-x-100` underline span (the active "Home" link correctly has no such span, since it uses its own `after:` pseudo-element underline instead), and the logo link carries `hover:scale-105`. Derivation: confirmed via `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`'s Guest Fee Rule ("₱150/hr per additional guest, booker is exempt from their own guest fee") and `src/lib/booking-pricing.ts`'s `guestFeeCentavos = guestCount * guestFeeRule.amountCentavos` that `guestCount` means *additional guests beyond the booker*, not total headcount — so total pax = 1 (booker) + `guestCount`, capped at the 2-pax tier since no 3+ rate exists. Since only a 1-pax/2-pax split exists, this collapses to: `guestCount === 0` → 1 pax (₱800 court coaching), `guestCount >= 1` → 2 pax (₱1,200) — verified live by moving guest count 0→1→0 and confirming Coaching's displayed price flipped ₱800→₱1,200→₱800 each time. **Scope conflict surfaced and resolved with Arjay before implementation**: the prompt's Part 2 step 5 asked for AddOnsStep's own Back/Continue buttons to be restyled (ghost Back, Hero-style Continue) — but Back/Continue for steps 1–4 are actually rendered by one shared bar in `BookingForm.tsx` (out of scope to touch, and touching it would also restyle Sport/Court/DateTime, which the prompt explicitly forbade). - 2026-08-18 — SPORT/COURT/DATE & TIME brand tokens + pre-selection bugfix + shared Back/Continue bar restyle: `SportStep.tsx`, `CourtStep.tsx`, `DateTimeStep.tsx`, `Calendar.tsx`, `TimeSlotGrid.tsx`, and `BookingForm.tsx` touched (only the shared Back/Continue bar and the two state-initialization sites below in the last file) — `StepIndicator.tsx`, `AddOnsStep.tsx`, `ReviewStep.tsx`, `PaymentStep.tsx`, no API route, and no pricing logic were left untouched. **Part 1 root cause (confirmed by reading, not assumed)**: `BookingForm.tsx`'s data-load effect called `setResourceTypeId(json.resourceTypes[0].id)` on mount, and the resource-type-change effect called `setResourceId(selectedResourceType.resources[0]?.id ?? '')` — both initialized to the first array item rather than `null`/`''`, so `SportStep`/`CourtStep`'s `isSelected = id === propId` was true for the first card with zero clicks. Fixed both to initialize/reset to `''` instead (and the matching `handleStartOver` line, same bug pattern, in scope as "whichever state-initialization line turns out to cause Part 1's bug"). `canContinue` for steps 1–2 already gated on `!!resourceTypeId`/`!!resourceId`, so Continue is now correctly disabled until a real click happens (previously it was always enabled from load, since the auto-selected first item satisfied the check). Duration's 60-minute default (`DateTimeStep`) was left untouched — confirmed functionally required, since `TimeSlotGrid` needs a non-null duration to compute slot end-times/closing checks, and no `!!durationMinutes`-only gate exists to expose an "unselected" state cleanly. **Part 2/3 token sweep**: replaced all `border-black/[.145]` → `border-brand-dark/20`, selected outlined-control state → `border-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/5` (Sport/Court/Duration/Time-slot, matching `AddOnsStep`'s pattern), Calendar's selected date → `bg-brand-dark text-brand-light` (matching `StepIndicator`'s filled-circle treatment), past dates → `text-brand-dark/30` (was `text-zinc-400`/no distinction from future), future/available dates → `text-brand-dark` (was unset, relying on inherited color — this was the actual cause of "past vs. future indistinguishable," not a missing class), Time slots' disabled/exceeds-closing states → brand-dark/20 and amber tokens respectively (amber kept as the one non-brand accent, per existing convention), every `dark:` variant removed (none of these six files have a real dark theme — same root-cause class as the StepIndicator fix from earlier today), SPORT/COURT cards gained `bg-brand-light` fill. Shared Back/Continue bar in `BookingForm.tsx` now reuses `ReviewStep`'s ghost-button classes for Back and `Hero`'s button classes for Continue — same treatment SUMMARY already had, now consistent across all four steps that share this bar. **Self-caught regression during VERIFY**: my first pass at `Calendar.tsx`'s day-cell `className` used two independent ternary expressions — `isPast ? … : 'text-brand-dark …'` followed by a separate `isSelected ? 'bg-brand-dark text-brand-light …' : ''` — which both landed in the class list simultaneously on a selected date, recreating the exact conflicting-utility-class bug (`text-brand-dark` vs `text-brand-light` both present) this whole prompt exists to eliminate. Caught via `getComputedStyle` showing a selected date still rendering dark text, not cream, on click. Fixed by collapsing to one mutually-exclusive ternary chain (`isSelected` checked first, since a selected date is never past). `SportStep`/`CourtStep`/`TimeSlotGrid` were written as single ternary chains from the start and didn't have this issue — confirmed by reading each file's final class-string logic before verifying. **VERIFY environment note**: this session's Browser pane still can't composite frames (`computer{action:"screenshot"}`/`left_click` time out with "Browser pane is not displayed," same limitation noted in every entry since 2026-08-13) — but this time it also froze `transition-colors` mid-transition, so `getComputedStyle` read immediately after a `javascript_tool`-dispatched click sometimes returned the *pre-click* border/background color (confirmed via `requestAnimationFrame` hanging entirely — the compositor isn't running, so CSS transitions and rAF both stall). Worked around this by cross-checking with a rule-matching script (`element.matches(rule.selectorText)` walked recursively through `@layer` blocks, since Tailwind v4 nests utilities inside `@layer utilities` and a flat `cssRules` scan misses them) to confirm the *correct* rule — and no conflicting rule — actually targets each element, independent of paint state; DOM `className` strings and `get_page_text` (both paint-independent) were used for everything else. Mobile (375×812) first: confirmed via rule-matching that all 5 SPORT cards share identical unselected classes on fresh load (no pre-selection), Continue disabled; clicked Golf Simulator, confirmed via `className` it switched to `border-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/5` while the other 4 stayed on the unselected brand-dark/20 border; same pattern confirmed for COURT (Bay 1/Bay 2, no pre-selection, Continue disabled until click). DATE & TIME: Duration's 60-minute default confirmed selected (`aria-pressed="true"`, `border-accent-primary`) while 90-minute stayed on a visible `brand-dark/20` border; Calendar confirmed past dates (Aug 1–17) `aria-disabled="true"`/`cursor-not-allowed`/`brand-dark` at 30% opacity, today (Aug 18) and future dates full-opacity `brand-dark`, not disabled; clicked Aug 25 and confirmed (post-ternary-fix) `background-color: rgb(75, 46, 43)` / `color: rgb(253, 243, 231)` — legible, not cream-on-cream; Time grid rendered 33 slots all with visible `brand-dark/20` borders, clicked 9:00 AM and confirmed via rule-matching it resolved to `border-accent-primary`/`bg-accent-primary/5` with no `brand-dark` rule also matching. Shared Back/Continue bar confirmed identically classed at SPORT, COURT, DATE & TIME, and ADD-ONS. Desktop (1280×800) repeated the SPORT no-pre-selection and Golf-Simulator-selection checks with identical results. Regression: walked a full booking (Golf Simulator, Bay 1, Aug 25 9:00 AM, 60 min, no add-ons) through SUMMARY — all values carried through correctly (₱1,150.00 estimate matching golf-sim's 60-min non-member rate) — and clicked Confirm Booking, which created the hold (`POST /api/bookings` → `201 Created`) and advanced to the Payment page with Booking Reference `cmsxkehcx0001w47wzkzozsm7`, confirming no functional breakage from the class/state changes. `npx tsc --noEmit` passed clean. Grep confirmed zero `dark:`, `border-black`, `bg-foreground`, or `text-background` remain across all six files (one additional stray `dark:` was found and fixed on `BookingForm.tsx`'s "Loading booking form…" text — not part of the shared bar, but Part 2's token sweep is explicitly scoped to "all six files" and the dedicated grep-based VERIFY step requires zero matches, so this one-line text-color swap was included). `git status` confirmed only the six scoped files changed. **DB check**: queried `bookings` directly — the prior ReviewStep session's leftover hold (`cmsxiy9h40004w42g6e6vom5t`) is still `pending_payment` at ~41 minutes old (past the 10-minute `BOOKING_HOLD_MINUTES` window), unexpired because expire-on-write only fires when a *new* booking targets that same resource/slot and the daily cron sweep doesn't run in local dev — consistent with the existing hybrid-expiry design documented in CLAUDE.md, not a bug; several older stale holds from even earlier sessions are sitting in the same state for the same reason. This session's own new hold (`cmsxkehcx0001w47wzkzozsm7`) was 39 seconds old at last check, well inside its hold window — left as-is, same handling as every prior session's VERIFY-created hold, no new cleanup mechanism invented.

- 2026-08-18 — Prefetch `/api/resources` on `/book` page load instead of on gate-continue-click: `src/app/book/page.tsx`, `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx` touched only, per explicit scope — `AnnouncementGate.tsx` itself, `src/app/api/resources/route.ts`, `src/lib/prisma.ts`, and no other wizard step were touched. Root cause (measured, not assumed): the resources fetch previously lived inside `BookingForm.tsx`'s mount effect, so it only started once the user clicked past `AnnouncementGate` — temporary route instrumentation on `GET /api/resources` measured the actual Supabase round-trip at 1.3–4s per request in this environment, meaning every booking attempt paid that full latency as a visible "Loading booking form…" flash immediately after the gate, even though the gate itself is shown for several seconds first with nothing preventing the fetch from running underneath it. Not a query or index problem — the route does a single straightforward read; the latency is round-trip/connection overhead. **Part 1**: added a `useEffect` to `BookPage` (`src/app/book/page.tsx`) that fires the same fetch (same `cancelled`-flag cleanup-on-unmount pattern moved verbatim) unconditionally on mount, storing `data`/`loading`/`loadError` in `BookPage` state. **Part 2**: removed `BookingForm`'s internal fetch effect and its own `data`/`loadError` state entirely; `BookingForm` now accepts `data`, `loading`, `loadError` as props (`ResourcesResponse` type exported from `BookingForm.tsx` so `BookPage` can type its state without duplicating the interface) — same names it already used internally, no new naming invented. **Part 3**: `BookingForm`'s existing "Loading booking form…" fallback (`if (loading || !data) return …`) is unchanged in substance — since state is now lifted and passed as props, it naturally shows only when `BookingForm` mounts before the parent's fetch has resolved (fast-click case) and is skipped entirely when the fetch already resolved while the gate was showing (slow-click case), with no new local state needed to track this. VERIFY performed against Arjay's already-running dev server (port 3000) via the Browser pane — `computer{action:"screenshot"}` failed with the pane-not-displayed limitation, but unlike several recent sessions, `computer{action:"left_click"}`/`read_page`/`get_page_text`/`read_network_requests` all worked normally this session, so verification used real clicks plus `javascript_tool` only for the sub-second timing probe below. Slow-click test: reloaded `/book`, waited several seconds on the gate, clicked Continue — confirmed via `get_page_text` the SPORT step (all 5 resource-type cards with correct prices, e.g. "Pickleball Court — 3 Courts — ₱650/hr") rendered immediately with no loading text ever observed. Fast-click test: used a `javascript_tool`-dispatched click plus a 20ms-interval polling loop (`GATE`/`LOADING`/`WIZARD` text markers) to click as close to page-mount as tooling allows and sample state every 20ms — in this local environment the fetch consistently resolved in 20–100ms (not the 1.3–4s measured against the deployed/Supabase-pooled path), so the loading branch was never caught mid-render in this session; confirmed instead that the transition from GATE to WIZARD is always clean (no flash of stale/empty content, no hang) at the fastest click timing achievable here. **Network throttling (Slow 3G) was not run** — neither the Browser pane's tool surface nor the available Playwright MCP tools expose network-condition emulation, and route-instrumentation-based delay injection was ruled out as out-of-scope (would have required touching `route.ts`); flagged rather than skipped silently. Confirmed via `read_network_requests` exactly 2 requests to `/api/resources` fire per page load in dev — expected React StrictMode double-invoke, not a regression (per the originating prompt's own allowance). Regression: walked a full booking (Pickleball Court → Court 1 → Aug 20, 9:00 AM, 60 min → Add-Ons (no add-ons) → SUMMARY, ₱650.00) through to Confirm Booking, which created the hold (`POST /api/bookings` → `201 Created`) and landed on the Payment page with Booking Reference `cmsyfxmez0001w488w9hs0akt` shown immediately — no regression to any downstream step from lifting the fetch. `npx tsc --noEmit` passed clean. **One real `pending_payment` hold was created during this VERIFY pass** (`cmsyfxmez0001w488w9hs0akt`, Pickleball Court, Court 1, 2026-08-20 9:00 AM, ₱650.00) — added to Next Up's existing stray-test-data list rather than deleted, same handling as every prior session's unavoidable VERIFY holds.

This will affect every future migration on this project going forward — flagging for awareness, not fixed as part of this prompt (out of scope). One new dependency: `next-auth@beta` (v5, JWT sessions only) — confirmed via `git diff --stat` that `package.json`/`package-lock.json` show only this addition. New `src/lib/admin-auth.ts` (`hashPassword`/`verifyPassword`, Node's built-in `crypto.scrypt` + `timingSafeEqual`, no new dependency). New root `auth.ts` (Credentials provider, `authorize` looks up `AdminUser` by email via the shared Prisma singleton, returns `null` on any failure — missing user or wrong password — without distinguishing which) and `src/app/api/auth/[...nextauth]/route.ts` exporting `{ GET, POST }`. **Edge Runtime split (found during VERIFY, not anticipated in the original prompt)**: `middleware.ts` runs on Next.js's Edge Runtime by default, which can't load Node's `crypto` module — importing the full `auth.ts` (Credentials provider → `admin-auth.ts` → `crypto`) directly into `middleware.ts` compiled with a "Node.js module loaded in Edge Runtime" warning that silently tolerated in Turbopack dev but would hard-fail on a real Edge deployment. Fixed with the standard Auth.js v5 split: new `auth.config.ts` (edge-safe, providers: [], just `session`/`pages`) is spread into `auth.ts`'s full config, and `middleware.ts` builds its own lightweight `NextAuth(authConfig).auth` wrapper instead of importing `auth.ts` directly — confirmed via a dev-server restart that the Edge warning is gone and `/admin` still correctly 307s to `/admin/login`. New root `middleware.ts`, matcher `/admin/:path*`, allows `/admin/login` through unauthenticated and redirects everything else under `/admin` to it when `req.auth` is absent — confirmed the matcher doesn't touch `/book`, `/`, or any `/api/*` route. New `src/app/admin/login/page.tsx` (server action calling `signIn('credentials', formData)`, `AuthError` caught and redirected back with `?error=1`, one generic "Invalid email or password." line, no field-specific hint) and `src/app/admin/page.tsx` ("Signed in as {email}" + sign-out server action calling `signOut()`), both plain/unstyled per scope. The reschedule form (`src/components/admin/RescheduleForm.tsx`) is a small client component using `fetch(..., {method:'PATCH'})` against the Part 5 route and `router.refresh()` on success — chosen over a server action because the existing CLAUDE.md convention ("Client components that call these routes branch on `res.status`") already assumes this pattern, and a real PATCH to the documented route is what VERIFY exercises. **Reschedule route (Part 5)**: `src/app/api/admin/bookings/[id]/reschedule/route.ts`, validation order exactly as specified (401 → 404 → 400 not-confirmed → 400 out-of-hours → transaction), reuses `isWithinBusinessHours`, catches the `booking_no_overlap` exclusion-constraint violation for a 409, single `$transaction` capturing original start/end before updating the booking and appending a `BookingReschedule` row with `performedById` from the real session.
