- 2026-08-21 — `Bulletin` model extended to a unified schema, matching News/AnnouncementGate's already-established field shape (schema + infrastructure only, no CRUD, no component changes). `prisma/schema.prisma`: added `excerpt` (String, required), `category` (new `BulletinCategory` enum — `Renovation`/`Closure`/`Tournament`/`Community`, exact casing matched to `NewsGrid.tsx`'s `NewsCategory` literal union so no display-string mapping layer is needed), `imageUrl` (String, required, `@map("image_url")`), `socialPlatform`/`socialUrl` (both `String?`, `@map`'d). `publishedAt` deliberately left `DateTime?` — not made required at the schema level, per explicit instruction (a bulletin can exist as an unpublished draft with no publish date; "every published row has a real date" is an application-logic rule for a future prompt, not a NOT NULL constraint). The normal `prisma migrate dev` flow hit the same shadow-DB failure CLAUDE.md already documents (an earlier migration inserts into Supabase-managed `storage.buckets`, which doesn't exist on a fresh shadow DB — this blocks shadow-DB replay for *any* new migration once that migration is in history, not just ones that themselves touch `storage.buckets`) — so, per the documented workaround, the column/enum migration was hand-written (`prisma/migrations/20260821010000_extend_bulletin_model/migration.sql`) and applied via `prisma migrate deploy`, which skips the shadow database. Second part: a new public `bulletin-images` Supabase Storage bucket was provisioned, mirroring the private `membership-applications` bucket's migration structure (`prisma/manual-sql/bulletin-images-bucket.sql`, deliberately kept isolated from the `prisma migrate dev`-generated folder, same convention as `booking-exclusion-constraint.sql`) — `public = true` (the one deliberate difference from the reference bucket), one new RLS policy (`public_read_bulletin_images`, SELECT, `anon`+`authenticated`, scoped to `bucket_id = 'bulletin-images'`) for public read access; no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy was added, mirroring the reference bucket's pattern of relying on default-deny RLS for non-service roles rather than an explicit write-restriction policy. Applied via its own hand-written migration folder + `prisma migrate deploy`, same shadow-DB-bypass reasoning as above. No new env var needed — `src/lib/supabase-storage.ts`'s existing `SUPABASE_URL`/`SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` pair already parameterizes the bucket name per call. VERIFY: a disposable Prisma script (`information_schema.columns` + `pg_enum` query, deleted after use) confirmed all 5 new `bulletins` columns with correct types/nullability and `BulletinCategory` with exactly the 4 expected values, no more/fewer; `Bulletin` row count confirmed still 0 (no CRUD exists yet to have created any). Via Supabase MCP: `bulletin-images` confirmed `public = true`; `membership-applications` confirmed still present, `public = false`, unchanged; `bulletin-images`'s policy list confirmed to contain exactly the one expected public-read policy; `membership-applications` confirmed to still have zero policies (its pre-migration state — it relies entirely on default-deny RLS + service-role bypass, same pattern this bucket's write side now also relies on); `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint reconfirmed present via `pg_constraint`. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (Prisma client regenerated after the dev server, which held the query-engine DLL locked, was stopped — **Arjay will need to run `npm run dev` again** to bring it back up). `git status` confirmed only `prisma/schema.prisma`, the two new migration folders, `prisma/manual-sql/bulletin-images-bucket.sql`, and this file changed — no component, page, or API route touched; `NEWS_ITEMS`/`SAMPLE_NOTICES` untouched.

- 2026-08-21 — Bulletin admin CRUD fully wired, replacing the read-only frame from the prior pass with real Create/Edit/Delete and image upload, closing out the admin side of the Bulletin feature. `src/lib/supabase-storage.ts` gained one thin addition, `getPublicUrl(bucket, path)`, constructing Supabase's public-object URL pattern — `uploadToStorage`/`deleteFromStorage` reused verbatim, unchanged. Two new API routes follow the established admin-route shape (`auth()` → 401, `findUnique` existence check → 404 on the `[id]` route, parse → 400, mutate → 200/201), each self-contained with its own local validators rather than a shared helper file, matching `resources/route.ts` vs. `resources/[id]/route.ts`'s existing convention: `POST /api/admin/bulletin` validates `title`/`excerpt`/`body` (non-empty), `category` (exactly one of the 4 enum values), `isPublished` (the literal string `'true'`/`'false'`, set explicitly by the client rather than relying on native-checkbox on/off semantics), `socialPlatform`/`socialUrl` (must be provided together, platform must be `instagram`/`facebook`), and a required `image` file (JPEG/PNG, ≤5MB — same limits as the membership-application upload, not loosened) — uploads the image first (path `{uuid}/image.{ext}`, a client-generated id also used as the `Bulletin.id` so the path is known before insert, same technique `membership-applications/route.ts` already uses), then creates the row with `publishedAt` server-computed (`new Date()` if publishing, `null` otherwise — never accepted from the client). `PATCH /api/admin/bulletin/[id]` reuses the same field validation, treats `image` as optional (uploads a new `{id}/image-{timestamp}.{ext}` object and swaps `imageUrl` only if a file was actually provided, otherwise leaves the existing `imageUrl` untouched), and best-effort-deletes the old Storage object after a successful swap (log-and-continue, never blocking the response — `deleteFromStorage` already does this internally). `DELETE /api/admin/bulletin/[id]` deletes the row then best-effort-deletes its Storage image the same way. **`publishedAt` logic, the trickiest part of this slice**: the correct trigger for auto-setting `publishedAt` to `new Date()` is "this bulletin has never been published before" (`existing.publishedAt === null`), not "was just unpublished" (`existing.isPublished === false`) — an initial implementation used the latter and was caught live during VERIFY (see below): unpublishing then republishing the same bulletin incorrectly bumped `publishedAt` to a new timestamp instead of preserving the original one, exactly the regression the spec called out by name. Fixed by keying the check on `existing.publishedAt` instead of `existing.isPublished`, then re-verified the unpublish→republish cycle to confirm the date now genuinely holds. `src/components/admin/BulletinFormModal.tsx` (new) follows `ResourceFormModal.tsx`/`PriceEditModal.tsx`'s conventions exactly (`Modal.tsx` wrapper, `mode: 'add' | 'edit'`, form mounted only while open, no `useEffect` reset) — Title/Excerpt/Body/Category/Image/Social Platform/Social URL/Published fields, a local `URL.createObjectURL` preview for a newly chosen file (falling back to the existing `imageUrl` in edit mode), client-side pre-submit checks mirroring the server's rules for immediate feedback (server 400 remains the source of truth, not skipped), and a `FormData` submit via `fetch` with no manually-set `Content-Type` (left to the browser for correct multipart boundaries) — on failure the server's `error` string is shown inline and every entered field is preserved, never reset. `src/components/admin/BulletinList.tsx` (new, `'use client'`) is the extracted interactive list — same page.tsx-stays-server / interactive-bits-extracted-to-a-client-component split `resources/page.tsx` → `ResourcesTabs.tsx` already established — receiving the server-fetched `bulletins` array as a prop, owning the "+ Add Bulletin" button and per-row Edit/Delete (delete uses the same `window.confirm(...)` + `DELETE` + `router.refresh()` pattern as Resource delete), with one shared `BulletinFormModal` instance toggled via `addModalOpen`/`editingBulletin` state (same sharing pattern `ResourceTypeCard` uses). `src/app/admin/(protected)/bulletin/page.tsx` is now a thin server component (just the `prisma.bulletin.findMany` call, the page heading, and an updated italic helper note confirming Add/Edit/Delete are live) rendering `<BulletinList bulletins={bulletins} />` — no `'use client'` needed there anymore. No schema changes, no other admin route/page touched. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean both before and after the `publishedAt` fix; scoped `npx eslint` on every new/touched file shows only the same pre-existing `@next/next/no-img-element` warning tolerated elsewhere in this admin panel (not suppressed). **VERIFY**, all live against the dev server via the Browser pane (this environment's file-picker gap — same one documented in the 2026-08-20 Membership-application entry — worked around by base64-encoding two disposable local test PNGs and injecting them into the file inputs via a script-driven `DataTransfer`/`File` construction, same technique as before), logged in as `winstonsipandserve@gmail.com`: created a fully-filled published bulletin (Instagram social link included) — got 201, `publishedAt` auto-set to ~now, the image's real public Supabase URL rendered and loaded (`naturalWidth` confirmed non-zero) in the list; created a second bulletin with `isPublished: false` — confirmed `publishedAt: null` and the gray Draft pill; edited the draft to published — `publishedAt` became non-null; edited the same row again touching only the excerpt — `publishedAt` unchanged, `updatedAt` bumped, confirming the "stays true" branch doesn't overwrite; unpublished it — `publishedAt` preserved, not cleared; republished it — **this is where the `existing.isPublished`-vs-`existing.publishedAt` bug above was caught and fixed**, then the same unpublish→republish cycle was re-run and `publishedAt` was confirmed genuinely unchanged this second time. Replaced the first bulletin's image via edit — confirmed via Supabase MCP that `imageUrl` pointed at the new object and the old one was gone from `storage.objects` (both this replacement and the accidental one below cleaned up correctly, confirming the replace-and-delete-old-image path works in both cases). Validation, all via direct `fetch` bypassing the client (server-side validation confirmed independent of client trust): missing title → 400 `"Title is required"`; `socialPlatform` set with no `socialUrl` → 400 `"socialPlatform and socialUrl must be provided together"`; an invalid category string sent directly against the API → 400, confirming the client `<select>`'s options aren't the only enforcement. Also confirmed via the actual modal: submitting with a blank title (excerpt/body/category filled) was caught client-side before any request fired, the inline error showed, and every other field's value survived. Auth: unauthenticated `POST /api/admin/bulletin` and `PATCH`/`DELETE /api/admin/bulletin/[id]` all 401'd; an authenticated request against a nonexistent id 404'd on both `PATCH` and `DELETE`. Deleted both test bulletins via the UI's Delete button (`window.confirm` auto-accepted for the scripted test) — confirmed via Supabase MCP that both rows and both Storage objects were gone, `bulletins` back to 0 rows, `bulletin-images` back to 0 objects (**test data was cleaned up, no seed content was intentionally kept**). Regression: `/admin`, `/admin/bookings`, `/admin/resources`, `/admin/memberships` all reloaded and rendered correctly with no new console errors beyond the ones caused by this session's own deliberate 400/401/404 VERIFY calls; `/news` and `/book` were not touched and not re-verified, per scope. `git status` confirmed only the files named in scope (plus `PROGRESS.md`/`PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`) changed.

## Next Up

- Core booking + payment flow is now a two-phase hold-then-attach model: SPORT → COURT → DATE & TIME → ADD-ONS → SUMMARY (Confirm Booking creates the hold, `customerId: null`) → Payment page (Booking Reference shown immediately; Name/Contact/Email + Pay Now attaches the customer and re-prices, with a confirmation step if the price changed) → PayMongo Checkout (auto-redirect) → `/book/confirmation`. Before the PayMongo→app redirect leg actually works, fix the `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=""` misconfiguration (still present, unaffected by this prompt) and restart the dev server; separately, point the PayMongo Dashboard webhook at whichever URL should receive live traffic (currently pointed at the Vercel deployment) if local webhook testing without the self-signed-webhook workaround is wanted.
- Marketing site: **Home is now fully done.** Its layout is verified against a real mock UI (`localhost:3001`), not just a text-prompt description — Hero, StatsBar, How It Works, the new Two Sides section, Facilities, and CtaBanner all match the mock's structure/spacing/copy/icons/hover-behavior section-for-section, with colors deliberately kept on our own locked brown/cream tokens rather than the mock's different (green/maroon) palette, per Arjay's explicit choice — see the two 2026-08-20 mock-diffing Completed Log entries (the second is the corrective pass; the first's "Facilities already matches" claim was wrong and has been superseded). Navbar floats transparent-with-light-text over Hero at the top of the page and transitions to its solid/dark-text appearance on scroll (`fixed`, not `sticky`, so the transparent state actually overlaps Hero), with that transition hardened so the background/logo genuinely interpolate rather than snap. TwoSides carries a responsive day-to-night gradient background (`brand-light`→`brand-dark`, vertical on mobile/horizontal on desktop) with the "At the Bar" column recolored for contrast against its dark end. Every CTA button across Navbar/Hero/CtaBanner (7 instances) went through two same-day style passes — a gradient-fill/colored-shadow/lift/arrow-motion treatment, then a restraint pass back to flat single-tone fills — before this session's final radius/size/legibility pass closed it out: all 7 now use a subtler `rounded-lg` (was `rounded-full`), Navbar's two buttons shrunk to a more proportionate `py-2.5`/trimmed horizontal padding (Hero's and CtaBanner's sizes untouched), and "Become a Member" now switches to light `accent-light` text/border when the header is transparent, reverting to `brand-dark`/`brand-mid` once scrolled — see the latest Completed Log entry. The Navbar scroll transition's smooth playback is still only verified at the CSS-mechanics/className level, not watched running, due to this environment's ongoing Browser-pane non-compositing limitation (documented since 2026-08-13) — worth a quick manual look from Arjay. The one remaining staged item on Home is a Facilities photography swap (real Café & Bar and Speakeasy Lounge photos), still pending from Arjay — no further copy/color/layout/button work needed there. **A mobile-responsiveness audit (audit-only) has since been completed** — see the 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry and `docs/audits/2026-08-21-home-mobile-audit.md` — surfacing one blocking issue (TwoSides' "Book a Court →" link unreadable against its own mobile gradient at 320/375/414/639px) and four polish-level findings; a dedicated fix pass against that doc is next up (see below), not yet started. **Next remaining marketing work**: Café & Bar is now done (`/cafe-bar` — see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry; placeholder photos and TBD hours are known follow-ups, not blockers). Membership is now done too — `/membership` is an info page (hero, tier cards, application-process steps, Apply CTA) and `/membership/apply` holds the relocated application form (see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry, "Membership page split"). About is now done too — `/about` (Hero, Our Story, Values, dual-CTA), see the 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry — Our Story's copy is generic placeholder pending the real founding story from Arjay/client, same category of flag as Footer's placeholder contact info and Home's placeholder Hero copy. **News is now done too, closing out the last remaining marketing page** (see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry). Book Now's own visual pass is complete end-to-end — the `/book` page heading/subtext, `AnnouncementGate`, `StepIndicator`, `AddOnsStep`, `ReviewStep` (SUMMARY), `SportStep`/`CourtStep`/`DateTimeStep`/`Calendar`/`TimeSlotGrid`, and `PaymentStep` are all on-theme, and the shared Back/Continue bar (`BookingForm.tsx`) that steps 1–4 use matches SUMMARY's buttons. **No step in the booking wizard is on unthemed `foreground`/`zinc`/`black`/`dark:` styling anymore — the entire booking flow, hold-to-payment, is on brand tokens.** News is now done too (`/news` — see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry, fully static/hardcoded content, DB shape deliberately not yet designed) — **all 6 marketing pages are now built.** Navbar/Footer are still composed per-page rather than a shared layout — worth noting Navbar is `fixed` (overlaying whatever comes first on the page) rather than `sticky`, so any future page reusing it needs a hero-like top section or its own top padding to avoid content being covered at scroll position 0. - Admin panel: bookings list/detail/reschedule, memberships list/detail (read-only frame), and now Resources & Pricing (tabbed Courts/Simulators/Guest Fee — Resource-level Add/Edit/Delete **and** Pricing/Add-on/Guest-Fee edit are now live; see 2026-08-19 and 2026-08-20 Completed Log entries) are all live on top of the login gate, and the nav surfaces all 5 sections (Dashboard/Bookings/Resources & Pricing/Memberships/Bulletin) with active-tab highlighting — Bulletin (`/admin/bulletin`) is now also a live read-only frame (see 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry), so every nav section is past the Coming Soon stage, gated by the same `(protected)/layout.tsx` check. The Memberships detail page (`/admin/memberships/[id]`) and Resources & Pricing (`/admin/resources`) have both had a Tailwind bento visual pass (status/active pills, bordered cards, neutral grays); `bookings/[id]/page.tsx` remains the one detail page still on the plain-inline-style convention and hasn't had an equivalent pass yet. Still unbuilt: Pricing/Add-on/Guest-Fee create + delete (the three models' `+ Add` buttons and any row-delete affordance are still disabled — the 2026-08-20 slice was edit-only by design), password reset, additional roles, rate-limiting. Bulletin CRUD is no longer on this list — it's now fully live (see the final 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry). The 2026-08-20 Pricing/Add-on/Guest-Fee edit slice has now been fully verified, including the actual browser click-through (done from the planning Project via an already-authenticated session, not by Claude Code — see the follow-up Completed Log entry) — no remaining verification gap for this slice. The two manual steps previously blocking the login gate are now resolved *for local dev*: `AUTH_SECRET` was empty in `.env.local` and has been set to a real generated value (done as part of an earlier session's VERIFY, since it was a hard blocker — not a decision, just a missing random value); a working `AdminUser` now exists (`claude-verify-admin@example.com`, a disposable test account created for VERIFY — see Completed Log; its own `BookingReschedule` audit row was removed along with every other row in that table by the 2026-08-19 stray-Booking-data cleanup, so that account no longer has any dependent rows of its own either). **Arjay still needs to**: (1) confirm the `AUTH_SECRET` value now in `.env.local` is fine to keep or swap it for his own, (2) do the same `AUTH_SECRET` generation on `staging`/`main`'s own env vars before promoting, since `.env.local` is local-only. A real `AdminUser` account for `winstonsipandserve@gmail.com` (password `password123`) already exists — created 2026-08-18 in an earlier unlogged run of the now-deleted `prisma/bootstrap-admin.ts` (see 2026-08-19 Completed Log entry) — distinct from the disposable `claude-verify-admin@example.com` test account referenced above. A genuine, previously-undiagnosed anomaly was also found and later confirmed broader than first scoped: `middleware.ts` does not appear to execute at all in this Next 16/Turbopack-on-Windows dev setup — not just for the `/api/admin/:path*` reschedule route originally flagged, but for `/admin/*` page routes too (see both 2026-08-19 Completed Log entries) — worth a dedicated look, though not a live security gap today since every gated route has its own working defense-in-depth check (`(protected)/layout.tsx`'s `auth()` for pages, each `/api/admin/*` route's own 401 check). Any new route added under the middleware matcher without its own auth check would currently be unprotected locally — worth re-verifying against `staging` before assuming this is dev-only.
- **Regional-latency question, non-blocking, now scoped to `/api/resources` only**: Home is no longer part of this question at all — it has zero DB calls now (see 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry). The remaining open question is just `/api/resources`'s own round-trip cost as consumed by `/book`: the resources-prefetch pass measured it at 1.3–4s per request via temporary route instrumentation (root cause for lifting the fetch earlier in the page load — see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry), but the same fetch resolved in 20–100ms when re-tested live in an earlier session's Browser-pane walkthrough against the same local dev server. Worth a dedicated look at whether this is a Supabase region (`ap-southeast-2`) vs. dev-environment/connection-pooling difference, or just measurement-run variance — not urgent since the prefetch change is a net win either way, but worth understanding before assuming the 1.3–4s figure represents steady-state production latency.
- **`bookings_customer_id_fkey` schema drift, non-blocking**: while applying the membership-applications-bucket migration (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry), `prisma migrate dev --create-only` detected that the live `bookings_customer_id_fkey` constraint doesn't match what `schema.prisma` implies for the nullable `customerId` — missing `ON DELETE SET NULL`/`ON UPDATE CASCADE`. Left untouched deliberately (out of scope for that task, which only needed to create a storage bucket) — worth a dedicated look at whether the live constraint should be brought in line with schema.prisma's default referential action, or whether schema.prisma should be adjusted to match a deliberate choice made when `customerId` was made nullable (see the `make_booking_customer_id_nullable` migration, 2026-08-12).
- **Member-repricing (`priceUpdate` notice) still unverified live**: the `memberships` table currently has zero rows with `status = 'active'` and a future `end_date`, so `PATCH /api/bookings/[id]`'s member-discount branch (and PaymentStep's restyled "your final price is X, was Y" notice) has never been exercised against real data in this environment — only verified by code inspection that the logic/JSX is unchanged. Worth seeding one active test membership the next time this path needs live verification.
- **Browser-pane verification limitation, expanded**: beyond the long-standing "Browser pane is not displayed" issue (screenshots/clicks time out, documented since 2026-08-13), this session found the pane also isn't compositing frames at all — `requestAnimationFrame` never fires (hangs until timeout) and CSS `transition-colors` freezes mid-transition, so `getComputedStyle` read immediately after a click can report the *pre-click* color. Rule-matching (`element.matches(rule.selectorText)`, walked recursively through `@layer` blocks) sidesteps this since it doesn't depend on paint — worth reaching for first over `getComputedStyle` on any color/style check in future sessions until this environment issue is resolved.
- Bulletin: schema is unified (`excerpt`/`category`/`imageUrl`/`socialPlatform`/`socialUrl` + `BulletinCategory` enum, plus a public `bulletin-images` Storage bucket) and **`/admin/bulletin` now has full Create/Edit/Delete + image upload/replacement/cleanup, fully verified live** (see the final 2026-08-21 Completed Log entry) — the admin side of this feature is done. **The two remaining pieces are `/news` and `/book`'s `AnnouncementGate` frontend wiring** — both are still on hardcoded arrays (`NEWS_ITEMS`, the gate's 3 sample notices) shaped to match the `Bulletin` model's fields but not yet fetched from it; there is also still no public `GET` route for these pages to read from. This is the immediate next step for the Bulletin feature.
- Membership: application submission is live at `/membership` (see 2026-08-18 Completed Log entry), the admin review UI is live at `/admin/memberships`, and the approve/reject mutation itself is now wired (see 2026-08-20 Completed Log entry) — the tier-activation *payment* flow (an actual PayMongo charge for the ₱5,500/12,500/22,500 plan price, vs. today's free/manual approval that just creates the `Membership` row) is still open.
